# Full upstream changelog: Effect 4.0.6 → 4.1.0

Every release section for **4.0.7 and 4.1.0** from all package changelogs at
[`effect@4.1.0`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/tree/effect%404.1.0).
Source commit: `9a91b1926a5fbfaffd302a7a41a94d4d53a1154e`.
The interval excludes [`effect@4.0.6`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/tree/effect%404.0.6),
commit `3b28bf686bf26e9700edd39c025aff2744dc5303`.

All **66 package release sections across 33 packages** are retained verbatim
(apart from trailing whitespace normalization), including **31 dependency-only
sections**, contributor attribution, and upstream links. The other 35 sections
contain package-specific release notes, sometimes alongside dependency updates.
Packages are ordered by source path; releases within each package are newest
first. Later entries may supersede earlier behavior.

The final part also retains the **two official GitHub release bodies** for
`effect@4.0.7` and `effect@4.1.0`, with their source URLs and publication times.
These bodies were retrieved from the Effect-TS/effect GitHub releases on
2026-10-29. After trailing whitespace normalization, each is identical to the
corresponding `packages/effect/CHANGELOG.md` section without its version heading.
They contain no separate release overview; no overview has been invented. The
two official bodies are additional copies, not part of the 66 package-section
count. GitHub release metadata is external to the tagged source tree.

# packages/ai/anthropic/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`38f96d8`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/38f96d8154fa7deb5ce1be0cd9a63ad0e7ef39c6) Thanks @effect-bot! - Regenerate the unstable `Generated` module from the pinned anthropic-sdk-typescript 0.135.0 spec, so newer request settings such as adaptive thinking `display` can be configured. Every released `Generated` export name still exists. The released Skills schemas, such as `Skill` and `CreateSkillResponse`, keep their released definitions. Other released types follow the current API: they gain fields and union members, and fields added since the release stay optional, so older responses still decode. The Files and Skills changes below are the exception: they follow Anthropic's migration of those APIs to their GA shapes and do not decode the previous beta shapes.

  ### Breaking changes

  - The Skills client methods decode the current API shapes, exported under new `*Resource` names. No method decodes with the released Skills schemas.
    - `createSkillV1SkillsPost`, `getSkillV1SkillsSkillIdGet` and their `beta*` variants return `SkillResource` / `BetaSkillResource`. Read `display_name` instead of `display_title`, and `latest_version_id` instead of `latest_version`.
    - `createSkillVersionV1SkillsSkillIdVersionsPost`, `getSkillVersionV1SkillsSkillIdVersionsVersionGet` and their `beta*` variants return `SkillVersionResource` / `BetaSkillVersionResource`, which no longer include `directory` or `version`.
    - The Skills list methods return `List*ResourcesResponse`. Page with `next_page` instead of `has_more`.
    - The delete methods and Skill version results type `type` as the literal the API always sends, such as `"skill_deleted"` and `"skill_version"`, instead of `string`.
  - The Files list methods `listFilesV1FilesGet` and `betaListFilesV1FilesGet` page with `page` and `next_page`. Their params no longer include `before_id` or `after_id`, and `FileListResponse` / `BetaFileListResponse` no longer include `has_more`, `first_id` or `last_id`. Pass the previous response's `next_page` as `page` to fetch the next page.
  - When migrating to the current Files and Skills shapes, remove the dated `files-api-2025-04-14` and `skills-2025-10-02` tokens from any `anthropic-beta` header you send, and keep any other beta tokens. The API still returns the previous beta shapes to requests that send these tokens, and the Files and Skills methods cannot decode them. See the upstream migration guides for [Files](https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/files#migrate-from-files-api-2025-04-14) and [Skills](https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/skills-guide#migrate-from-skills-2025-10-02).
  - Uploads take files instead of strings. `UploadFileV1FilesPostRequestFormData.file` and each Skills `files` entry are a `File` or `Blob`. Skills `files` is now required and non-null. As before, every file must be in one top-level directory that has a `SKILL.md`. The Skills create input renames `display_title` to `display_name`:

    ```ts
    const created = client.betaCreateSkillV1SkillsPost({
      payload: {
        display_name: "My skill",
        files: [new File([skillMd], "my-skill/SKILL.md", { type: "text/markdown" })]
      },
      config: undefined
    })
    ```

  - `BetaMessageIterationUsage.model` can be `null` as well as missing. Read it with a fallback, such as `usage.model ?? "unknown"`.
  - Text blocks in `BetaResponseMCPToolResultBlock` content have optional `citations`. Read them with a fallback, such as `part.citations ?? []`.
  - Response unions gain members. Exhaustive `switch`es without a default need new cases:
    - web fetch `error_code` adds `"content_too_large"`;
    - stop reasons add `"model_context_window_exceeded"`;
    - beta content blocks, server tool names and iteration usage add advisor variants.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9861](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9861) [`4f0600e`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f0600e90b61040c75b4c5c5be940db1079dd5f4) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add a `toModelOutput` option to `Tool.make` and `Tool.dynamic` for sending text, images and files to the model in place of the serialized tool result. Anthropic and OpenAI Responses send native content blocks; OpenAI-compatible APIs and OpenRouter send text with a placeholder for each file, via the new `Prompt.toolModelOutputToText`.

  OpenAI Responses now sends PDF file parts given as base64, data URL or HTTP(S) URL strings instead of dropping them.

- [#9859](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9859) [`16321ff`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/16321ff12e3e99146e579e0b456b167be4e88dad) Thanks @nmnmcc! - Fail with an `UnsupportedSchemaError` that names the tool when its parameters do not produce a JSON Schema object, for example `Schema.Struct({})`.

- [#9920](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9920) [`0b65c9f`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/0b65c9f585efffa52af358216dcf615aed53e4b3) Thanks @Marve10s! - Fix `LanguageModel` responses reporting `finishReason: "unknown"` instead of `"length"` when OpenAI stops with `max_output_tokens` or Anthropic stops with `model_context_window_exceeded`. A truncated OpenAI response that contains a tool call now reports `"length"` and no longer runs the tool handler.

- [#9975](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9975) [`65e44bb`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/65e44bbc945d6e00d901f2131511eca763247964) Thanks @effect-bot! - Accept Anthropic-compatible responses that omit cache and service tier usage fields, and keep previously reported input and cache token counts when a streamed `message_delta` omits them.

- [#9919](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9919) [`122b210`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/122b21040bc85c1e1c0f428c0c8c80923e75732b) Thanks @Marve10s! - Fix `AnthropicLanguageModel` sending the custom name instead of the provider name for provider-defined tools in `tool_choice`, `oneOf` filtering and replayed tool calls. Also fix `AnthropicTool.ComputerUse_20241022` declaring the provider name `computer_use` instead of `computer`, which made its tool calls fail with `ToolNotFoundError`.

  - effect@4.0.7

# packages/ai/cloudflare/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3f8f359`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3f8f3595b06f144b109c47c0bbc8788262aedb21) Thanks @jem-computer! - Declare decision images as input fields with `Decision.Image`, and add `Decision.locate`, which answers with a bounding box around one target in an image field.

  - `DecideOptions.images` is removed. Add `Decision.Image` fields to the input schema and refer to each image by its path in instructions:

    ```ts
    // Before
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: "Checkout error", images: [screenshot] })
    // After
    const Triage = Decision.make({
      input: Schema.Struct({ report: Schema.String, screenshot: Decision.Image }),
      decisions
    })
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: { report: "Checkout error", screenshot } })
    ```

  - Custom providers receive `images` as `{ reference, path, image }`, with each image replaced by its reference in `state` and its data as a base64 string or `URL`.
  - `Decision.locate({ image: "photo", instructions })` takes a path that `Decision.make` checks against the input's image fields. Providers opt in with `supportsLocate: true`. `Decision.Any` and `DecisionModel.ProviderAnswer` gain locate variants, so exhaustive switches over them need a `Locate` case.
  - `TypeSafeDecisionModel` supports images, sent as assets keyed by reference, and sends locate decisions as Box questions, which need `jev-preview` or `jev-1.14.0`. OpenAI and Cloudflare label each image with its reference.
- Updated dependencies [[`631d4f4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/631d4f41e2c09ce039cddad3f460a4793e53989d), # packages/ai/openai-compat/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.
- Updated dependencies [[`631d4f4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/631d4f41e2c09ce039cddad3f460a4793e53989d), ## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9861](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9861) [`4f0600e`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f0600e90b61040c75b4c5c5be940db1079dd5f4) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add a `toModelOutput` option to `Tool.make` and `Tool.dynamic` for sending text, images and files to the model in place of the serialized tool result. Anthropic and OpenAI Responses send native content blocks; OpenAI-compatible APIs and OpenRouter send text with a placeholder for each file, via the new `Prompt.toolModelOutputToText`.

  OpenAI Responses now sends PDF file parts given as base64, data URL or HTTP(S) URL strings instead of dropping them.

- [#9859](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9859) [`16321ff`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/16321ff12e3e99146e579e0b456b167be4e88dad) Thanks @nmnmcc! - Fail with an `UnsupportedSchemaError` that names the tool when its parameters do not produce a JSON Schema object, for example `Schema.Struct({})`.

# packages/ai/openai/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3f8f359`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3f8f3595b06f144b109c47c0bbc8788262aedb21) Thanks @jem-computer! - Declare decision images as input fields with `Decision.Image`, and add `Decision.locate`, which answers with a bounding box around one target in an image field.

  - `DecideOptions.images` is removed. Add `Decision.Image` fields to the input schema and refer to each image by its path in instructions:

    ```ts
    // Before
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: "Checkout error", images: [screenshot] })
    // After
    const Triage = Decision.make({
      input: Schema.Struct({ report: Schema.String, screenshot: Decision.Image }),
      decisions
    })
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: { report: "Checkout error", screenshot } })
    ```

  - Custom providers receive `images` as `{ reference, path, image }`, with each image replaced by its reference in `state` and its data as a base64 string or `URL`.
  - `Decision.locate({ image: "photo", instructions })` takes a path that `Decision.make` checks against the input's image fields. Providers opt in with `supportsLocate: true`. `Decision.Any` and `DecisionModel.ProviderAnswer` gain locate variants, so exhaustive switches over them need a `Locate` case.
  - `TypeSafeDecisionModel` supports images, sent as assets keyed by reference, and sends locate decisions as Box questions, which need `jev-preview` or `jev-1.14.0`. OpenAI and Cloudflare label each image with its reference.

- [#10087](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10087) [`472c469`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/472c469ce125bc521f274dc4f5956cef5c26d430) Thanks @effect-bot! - Node.js 22.19.0 (required by `undici` 8) is now the minimum for `@effect/platform-node`, `@effect/platform-node-shared`, `@effect/opentelemetry` and `@effect/docgen`, up from 18.

  `NodeSocket` WebSocket clients now use Node's global `WebSocket` instead of `ws`, so client-only usage no longer bundles `ws`.

  **Breaking:** `ws` moved to a new `NodeWS` module.

  - `NodeSocket.NodeWS` and `BunSocket.NodeWS` are removed. Import `NodeWS` from `@effect/platform-node` or `@effect/platform-node-shared` (on Bun, import `ws` directly). Use `NodeWS.WebSocket` in place of `NodeSocket.NodeWS.default`.
  - `NodeSocket.layerWebSocketConstructorWS` is removed. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor`.

  **Behaviour changes** for clients that pass handshake headers, which previously used `ws`. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor` to keep the old behaviour:

  - Reads cannot be paused. Incoming frames are buffered without limit, and a configured `highWaterMark` fails the socket with a `SocketReadError`.
  - Connection errors are a generic `ErrorEvent`, so `SocketOpenError` causes lack the underlying error code.
  - The `Socket.WebSocket` service is a global `WebSocket`, so `ws` `instanceof` checks and `ws`-only methods such as `terminate()` and `ping()` no longer apply.
  - A custom `Host` header is ignored.
  - Outgoing messages are not compressed. Incoming `permessage-deflate` messages are still decompressed.
  - Connections use undici's global dispatcher instead of `http.globalAgent` / `https.globalAgent`.
- Updated dependencies [[`631d4f4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/631d4f41e2c09ce039cddad3f460a4793e53989d), ## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9861](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9861) [`4f0600e`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f0600e90b61040c75b4c5c5be940db1079dd5f4) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add a `toModelOutput` option to `Tool.make` and `Tool.dynamic` for sending text, images and files to the model in place of the serialized tool result. Anthropic and OpenAI Responses send native content blocks; OpenAI-compatible APIs and OpenRouter send text with a placeholder for each file, via the new `Prompt.toolModelOutputToText`.

  OpenAI Responses now sends PDF file parts given as base64, data URL or HTTP(S) URL strings instead of dropping them.

- [#9859](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9859) [`16321ff`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/16321ff12e3e99146e579e0b456b167be4e88dad) Thanks @nmnmcc! - Fail with an `UnsupportedSchemaError` that names the tool when its parameters do not produce a JSON Schema object, for example `Schema.Struct({})`.

- [#9920](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9920) [`0b65c9f`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/0b65c9f585efffa52af358216dcf615aed53e4b3) Thanks @Marve10s! - Fix `LanguageModel` responses reporting `finishReason: "unknown"` instead of `"length"` when OpenAI stops with `max_output_tokens` or Anthropic stops with `model_context_window_exceeded`. A truncated OpenAI response that contains a tool call now reports `"length"` and no longer runs the tool handler.
- Updated dependencies [[`ac3485a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ac3485a0ad2c5aa0c3d2f2c9f1b6739cb05b2a4e), # packages/ai/openrouter/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9861](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9861) [`4f0600e`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f0600e90b61040c75b4c5c5be940db1079dd5f4) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add a `toModelOutput` option to `Tool.make` and `Tool.dynamic` for sending text, images and files to the model in place of the serialized tool result. Anthropic and OpenAI Responses send native content blocks; OpenAI-compatible APIs and OpenRouter send text with a placeholder for each file, via the new `Prompt.toolModelOutputToText`.

  OpenAI Responses now sends PDF file parts given as base64, data URL or HTTP(S) URL strings instead of dropping them.

- [#9859](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9859) [`16321ff`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/16321ff12e3e99146e579e0b456b167be4e88dad) Thanks @nmnmcc! - Fail with an `UnsupportedSchemaError` that names the tool when its parameters do not produce a JSON Schema object, for example `Schema.Struct({})`.

# packages/ai/typesafe/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3f8f359`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3f8f3595b06f144b109c47c0bbc8788262aedb21) Thanks @jem-computer! - Declare decision images as input fields with `Decision.Image`, and add `Decision.locate`, which answers with a bounding box around one target in an image field.

  - `DecideOptions.images` is removed. Add `Decision.Image` fields to the input schema and refer to each image by its path in instructions:

    ```ts
    // Before
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: "Checkout error", images: [screenshot] })
    // After
    const Triage = Decision.make({
      input: Schema.Struct({ report: Schema.String, screenshot: Decision.Image }),
      decisions
    })
    DecisionModel.decide(Triage, { input: { report: "Checkout error", screenshot } })
    ```

  - Custom providers receive `images` as `{ reference, path, image }`, with each image replaced by its reference in `state` and its data as a base64 string or `URL`.
  - `Decision.locate({ image: "photo", instructions })` takes a path that `Decision.make` checks against the input's image fields. Providers opt in with `supportsLocate: true`. `Decision.Any` and `DecisionModel.ProviderAnswer` gain locate variants, so exhaustive switches over them need a `Locate` case.
  - `TypeSafeDecisionModel` supports images, sent as assets keyed by reference, and sends locate decisions as Box questions, which need `jev-preview` or `jev-1.14.0`. OpenAI and Cloudflare label each image with its reference.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3c42058`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3c420582246e531eedefe0b98eede39dd9a98240) Thanks @effect-bot! - Remove `AiError.HttpRequestDetails` and `Response.HttpRequestDetails`, so AI errors and response metadata no longer carry request headers or query parameters. `AiError.NetworkError` records the request as `method` and `url` fields instead of `request`, and the `request` field is gone from `AiError.HttpContext` and `Response.ResponseMetadataPart`.

### Patch Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`7ea1ea2`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7ea1ea2ecfb1bb4808901c522259de44aaee6136) Thanks @wmaurer! - Add an optional `cause` to the AI `NetworkError`. It holds the `HttpClientError` the request failed with, stored unchanged and not redacted, so the reason a request failed is no longer lost. The new `NetworkError.fromHttpClientError` sets it, and all AI providers now use it. `NetworkError.fromRequestError` is unchanged.
- Updated dependencies [[`631d4f4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/631d4f41e2c09ce039cddad3f460a4793e53989d), ## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9993](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9993) [`f308290`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/f308290c49a2a6e16ebb8f1da13c3d46754c2fd4) Thanks @effect-bot! - Defer notifications from atoms set while React reads an atom during render, so initializing an atom that sets another atom no longer updates other components during render.
