# packages/platform/browser/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`8f23668`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8f236683ad92bc4b4f58e16e91d40ce713c20578) Thanks @fubhy! - Replace `effect/Crypto` with family-specific `effect/crypto/*` services, opaque keys, `CryptoError`, a configurable `WebCrypto` reference and key schemas in `effect/Schema`. Encoding secret or private keys through byte/format schemas requires `{ allowEncode: true }`.

  ### Breaking changes

  - Use `SecureRandom` for randomness and UUIDs, and `Digest` with algorithm values such as `Digest.sha256` for hashing. Replace `effect/testing/TestCrypto` with `Layer.mock` for the required services.
  - Provide crypto explicitly; it is no longer included in the runtime `*Services` bundles. `NodeCrypto`, `BunCrypto`, `DenoCrypto` and `BrowserCrypto` offer individual family layers and a combined `layer`, supplying `SecureRandom` where needed. Core modules offer `layerWebCrypto`.
  - Provide `Digest.Digest` instead of `Crypto.Crypto` to `SqlMessageStorage` and `SingleRunner`, and instead of `EventLogEncryption` to `SqlEventLogServerEncrypted.makeStorage` and `layerStorage`. Replace `EventLogEncryption.sha256String` with `digest.digestHex(Digest.sha256, bytes)`.

  Existing workflow ID, activity idempotency key and event log table-name encodings are unchanged.

  Event log encryption, identity key derivation and session authentication now run on `Aead`, `Digest`, `Signature` and `SecureRandom`. `EventLogEncryption.make` and `EventLogEncryption.layer` take those services from context, so Node apps can use the `NodeCrypto` layers. `EventLogEncryption.layerSubtle` and the session auth functions use the Web Crypto layers on the `WebCrypto` reference instead of `globalThis.crypto`. Existing identities, derived signing keys and encrypted batches are unchanged.

  UUIDv7 and ULID generation is now monotonic below the maximum 48-bit timestamp, including within a millisecond and across clock rollback. Ordering is per `SecureRandom` instance; the unsafe helpers and model UUIDv7 defaults share per-realm ordering.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`93895b9`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/93895b9899b3cc796663f64e3220aacb315a10e8) Thanks @tim-smart! - Add `keys` to `KeyValueStore` and `SchemaStore`. It returns a snapshot of the keys in the store, in no particular order, and is implemented by the memory, file system, SQL, Web Storage and IndexedDB stores. Custom stores built with `KeyValueStore.make` or `KeyValueStore.makeStringOnly` must now provide `keys`.

  `KeyValueStore.prefix(...)` now scopes `keys`, `size` and `isEmpty` to keys under the prefix, with the prefix removed from the returned keys. Previously `size` and `isEmpty` reported on the whole backing store.

  Stores built with `KeyValueStore.make` that don't provide `clearPrefix` now derive it from `keys` and `remove` instead of failing for non-empty prefixes.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`f97711f`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/f97711f43f24e4c5045f51bba373ba23ccf230fd) Thanks @effect-bot! - Isolate `Persistence` stores on the `KeyValueStore` and Redis backings. Clearing a store now only removes that store's entries, and different store / key pairs no longer map to the same backing key.

  Add `KeyValueStore.clearPrefix`, implemented by the memory, file system, SQL, Web Storage and IndexedDB stores. `KeyValueStore.prefix(...).clear` now only removes keys under the prefix. Stores built with `KeyValueStore.make` that don't provide `clearPrefix` remove the matching keys one by one instead of clearing everything.

  Both backings now store entries under `<storeId>:<key>`, with `%` and `:` in the store id percent-encoded.

  **Key layout change.** Entries written by earlier versions are not read back:

  - `Persistence.layerKvs` / `layerBackingKvs`: every existing entry becomes unreachable, because keys used to be `<storeId><key>` with no separator. Cached results are recomputed. `Chat.layerPersisted` on a `KeyValueStore` backing loses its stored chat histories.
  - `Persistence.layerRedis` / `layerBackingRedis`: keys are unchanged unless the store id contains `%` or `:`. Entries for those store ids become unreachable.

  Old entries are not migrated or removed automatically.

### Patch Changes

- [#10065](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10065) [`8e668fe`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8e668fe1ceb53846df37797d3df692e02bc3f7c5) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading UTF-8 BOM in URL-encoded body field names.

- [#10067](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10067) [`ce9e999`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ce9e9990546e38a0fadbc64240c2eedbb1c353d6) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading `?` in URL-encoded form field names. `urlParamsBody` previously dropped it as a query-string prefix, so `?name=value` decoded to `name`.

# packages/platform/bun/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`892fb79`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/892fb79e2dc711ef53025416b20a86f6164b39c2) Thanks @effect-bot! - `BunHttpServer` now disables Bun's 10 second `idleTimeout` unless you set one, so quiet response streams and slow uploads are no longer cut. Directly exposed servers should set `idleTimeout` to keep idle connections closed.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`8f23668`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8f236683ad92bc4b4f58e16e91d40ce713c20578) Thanks @fubhy! - Replace `effect/Crypto` with family-specific `effect/crypto/*` services, opaque keys, `CryptoError`, a configurable `WebCrypto` reference and key schemas in `effect/Schema`. Encoding secret or private keys through byte/format schemas requires `{ allowEncode: true }`.

  ### Breaking changes

  - Use `SecureRandom` for randomness and UUIDs, and `Digest` with algorithm values such as `Digest.sha256` for hashing. Replace `effect/testing/TestCrypto` with `Layer.mock` for the required services.
  - Provide crypto explicitly; it is no longer included in the runtime `*Services` bundles. `NodeCrypto`, `BunCrypto`, `DenoCrypto` and `BrowserCrypto` offer individual family layers and a combined `layer`, supplying `SecureRandom` where needed. Core modules offer `layerWebCrypto`.
  - Provide `Digest.Digest` instead of `Crypto.Crypto` to `SqlMessageStorage` and `SingleRunner`, and instead of `EventLogEncryption` to `SqlEventLogServerEncrypted.makeStorage` and `layerStorage`. Replace `EventLogEncryption.sha256String` with `digest.digestHex(Digest.sha256, bytes)`.

  Existing workflow ID, activity idempotency key and event log table-name encodings are unchanged.

  Event log encryption, identity key derivation and session authentication now run on `Aead`, `Digest`, `Signature` and `SecureRandom`. `EventLogEncryption.make` and `EventLogEncryption.layer` take those services from context, so Node apps can use the `NodeCrypto` layers. `EventLogEncryption.layerSubtle` and the session auth functions use the Web Crypto layers on the `WebCrypto` reference instead of `globalThis.crypto`. Existing identities, derived signing keys and encrypted batches are unchanged.

  UUIDv7 and ULID generation is now monotonic below the maximum 48-bit timestamp, including within a millisecond and across clock rollback. Ordering is per `SecureRandom` instance; the unsafe helpers and model UUIDv7 defaults share per-realm ordering.

- [#10087](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10087) [`472c469`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/472c469ce125bc521f274dc4f5956cef5c26d430) Thanks @effect-bot! - Node.js 22.19.0 (required by `undici` 8) is now the minimum for `@effect/platform-node`, `@effect/platform-node-shared`, `@effect/opentelemetry` and `@effect/docgen`, up from 18.

  `NodeSocket` WebSocket clients now use Node's global `WebSocket` instead of `ws`, so client-only usage no longer bundles `ws`.

  **Breaking:** `ws` moved to a new `NodeWS` module.

  - `NodeSocket.NodeWS` and `BunSocket.NodeWS` are removed. Import `NodeWS` from `@effect/platform-node` or `@effect/platform-node-shared` (on Bun, import `ws` directly). Use `NodeWS.WebSocket` in place of `NodeSocket.NodeWS.default`.
  - `NodeSocket.layerWebSocketConstructorWS` is removed. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor`.

  **Behaviour changes** for clients that pass handshake headers, which previously used `ws`. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor` to keep the old behaviour:

  - Reads cannot be paused. Incoming frames are buffered without limit, and a configured `highWaterMark` fails the socket with a `SocketReadError`.
  - Connection errors are a generic `ErrorEvent`, so `SocketOpenError` causes lack the underlying error code.
  - The `Socket.WebSocket` service is a global `WebSocket`, so `ws` `instanceof` checks and `ws`-only methods such as `terminate()` and `ping()` no longer apply.
  - A custom `Host` header is ignored.
  - Outgoing messages are not compressed. Incoming `permessage-deflate` messages are still decompressed.
  - Connections use undici's global dispatcher instead of `http.globalAgent` / `https.globalAgent`.

### Patch Changes

- [#10065](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10065) [`8e668fe`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8e668fe1ceb53846df37797d3df692e02bc3f7c5) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading UTF-8 BOM in URL-encoded body field names.

- [#10067](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10067) [`ce9e999`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ce9e9990546e38a0fadbc64240c2eedbb1c353d6) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading `?` in URL-encoded form field names. `urlParamsBody` previously dropped it as a query-string prefix, so `?name=value` decoded to `name`.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9751](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9751) [`fb38ce6`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/fb38ce6b01af86a0fb9ac1ee23600005c021cbf5) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow buffered payloads and typed `101` response headers on `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade` endpoints.

  - `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade({ protocol, headers })` declares `101` headers, encoded like `WithHeaders` headers. Clients decode them and return `{ headers, socket }`, and OpenAPI lists them on the `101`.
  - Upgrade endpoints can declare buffered payloads. Streaming payloads are still rejected.
  - Handlers receive an `upgrade` function instead of `socket`: `upgrade()`, or `upgrade({ headers })` when headers are declared.
  - `426 Upgrade Required` is returned before the payload is decoded.
  - `HttpServerRequest` gains `upgradeWith({ headers })`, and `upgrade` is `upgradeWith({})`. Third-party `HttpServerRequest` implementations must add it. WebSocket upgrades fail when headers are given.
  - `NodeHttpServer` waits for the request body to end before writing the `101`. On Node 25 and older, which do not parse upgrade request bodies, it reads `Content-Length` bodies from the connection and answers chunked ones with `411 Length Required`.
  - `NodeMultipart` fails with a `MultipartError` when its source stream errors, instead of waiting for more input.

- [#9828](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9828) [`45c467a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/45c467a106da66f785f2fd846aa3fad5c3dd2867) Thanks @tim-smart! - Add `Redis.reserve`, which leases a dedicated connection from a pool, so blocking commands like `XREAD BLOCK` and `BLPOP` no longer hold up other commands on the shared connection. `NodeRedis`, `BunRedis` and `DenoRedis` layers accept `maxConnections`, `minConnections`, `idleTimeout` and `acquireTimeout` to size the pool.

  `Redis.make` now requires a `connect` option, and hand-written `Redis` services need a `reserve` member.

# packages/platform/deno/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`8f23668`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8f236683ad92bc4b4f58e16e91d40ce713c20578) Thanks @fubhy! - Replace `effect/Crypto` with family-specific `effect/crypto/*` services, opaque keys, `CryptoError`, a configurable `WebCrypto` reference and key schemas in `effect/Schema`. Encoding secret or private keys through byte/format schemas requires `{ allowEncode: true }`.

  ### Breaking changes

  - Use `SecureRandom` for randomness and UUIDs, and `Digest` with algorithm values such as `Digest.sha256` for hashing. Replace `effect/testing/TestCrypto` with `Layer.mock` for the required services.
  - Provide crypto explicitly; it is no longer included in the runtime `*Services` bundles. `NodeCrypto`, `BunCrypto`, `DenoCrypto` and `BrowserCrypto` offer individual family layers and a combined `layer`, supplying `SecureRandom` where needed. Core modules offer `layerWebCrypto`.
  - Provide `Digest.Digest` instead of `Crypto.Crypto` to `SqlMessageStorage` and `SingleRunner`, and instead of `EventLogEncryption` to `SqlEventLogServerEncrypted.makeStorage` and `layerStorage`. Replace `EventLogEncryption.sha256String` with `digest.digestHex(Digest.sha256, bytes)`.

  Existing workflow ID, activity idempotency key and event log table-name encodings are unchanged.

  Event log encryption, identity key derivation and session authentication now run on `Aead`, `Digest`, `Signature` and `SecureRandom`. `EventLogEncryption.make` and `EventLogEncryption.layer` take those services from context, so Node apps can use the `NodeCrypto` layers. `EventLogEncryption.layerSubtle` and the session auth functions use the Web Crypto layers on the `WebCrypto` reference instead of `globalThis.crypto`. Existing identities, derived signing keys and encrypted batches are unchanged.

  UUIDv7 and ULID generation is now monotonic below the maximum 48-bit timestamp, including within a millisecond and across clock rollback. Ordering is per `SecureRandom` instance; the unsafe helpers and model UUIDv7 defaults share per-realm ordering.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`d2b6bc7`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/d2b6bc71ea63bc20f7efba052a95e71dc9853248) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Add experimental `Glob` path matching and a shared `FileSystem.glob` implementation for Node, Bun, and Deno.

  - Breaking: custom `FileSystem` implementations, including inputs to `FileSystem.make`, must provide `readDirectoryEntries`; mapping `readDirectory` names to `{ name, type: "Unknown" }` is supported. Wrappers must apply directory-listing changes to both methods.
  - `FileSystem.make` now derives `glob` instead of accepting it. Apply custom overrides afterward with `FileSystem.of({ ...fs, glob: customGlob })`.
  - Glob patterns use `/` separators and backslash escapes; Node extglobs no longer expand. Results use `/` separators and are relative to `root` unless the pattern is absolute. Deno wildcards now skip dotfiles unless explicitly named or `dot: true`; unreadable wildcard-reached directories are skipped, while read failures at the root or a leading literal directory propagate.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3d88452`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3d88452eb653a24fd8bd44894bedcdd8f3a09865) Thanks @fubhy! - Add `CommandLine` to `effect/process` for quoting and joining command lines, and support `Redacted` arguments and `env` values in `ChildProcess`.

  Migration:

  - `ChildProcess.make` templates keep quoted words together and throw a `CommandLineError` for shell syntax such as `|`, `$HOME` or `*`. Quote such characters or pass them with `${}`.
  - With the `shell` option, templates cannot contain `${}` values and extra arguments fail with `BadArgument`. Pass the whole script as the command, built with `CommandLine.join`.
  - `StandardCommand.args` and `env` values can be `Redacted` strings.
  - `ChildProcess.TemplateExpression` and `ChildProcess.TemplateExpressionItem` are removed. Use the `CommandLine` types of the same name.

### Patch Changes

- [#10065](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10065) [`8e668fe`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8e668fe1ceb53846df37797d3df692e02bc3f7c5) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading UTF-8 BOM in URL-encoded body field names.

- [#10067](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10067) [`ce9e999`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ce9e9990546e38a0fadbc64240c2eedbb1c353d6) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading `?` in URL-encoded form field names. `urlParamsBody` previously dropped it as a query-string prefix, so `?name=value` decoded to `name`.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9795](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9795) [`958307b`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/958307b689b03cdb2ce2d27eb9041a485dd2fac0) Thanks @effect-bot! - Fail `ChildProcess` exit codes when a `stdin` or `additionalFds` input stream fails, instead of reporting a successful exit after truncated input.

- [#9751](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9751) [`fb38ce6`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/fb38ce6b01af86a0fb9ac1ee23600005c021cbf5) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow buffered payloads and typed `101` response headers on `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade` endpoints.

  - `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade({ protocol, headers })` declares `101` headers, encoded like `WithHeaders` headers. Clients decode them and return `{ headers, socket }`, and OpenAPI lists them on the `101`.
  - Upgrade endpoints can declare buffered payloads. Streaming payloads are still rejected.
  - Handlers receive an `upgrade` function instead of `socket`: `upgrade()`, or `upgrade({ headers })` when headers are declared.
  - `426 Upgrade Required` is returned before the payload is decoded.
  - `HttpServerRequest` gains `upgradeWith({ headers })`, and `upgrade` is `upgradeWith({})`. Third-party `HttpServerRequest` implementations must add it. WebSocket upgrades fail when headers are given.
  - `NodeHttpServer` waits for the request body to end before writing the `101`. On Node 25 and older, which do not parse upgrade request bodies, it reads `Content-Length` bodies from the connection and answers chunked ones with `411 Length Required`.
  - `NodeMultipart` fails with a `MultipartError` when its source stream errors, instead of waiting for more input.

- [#9828](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9828) [`45c467a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/45c467a106da66f785f2fd846aa3fad5c3dd2867) Thanks @tim-smart! - Add `Redis.reserve`, which leases a dedicated connection from a pool, so blocking commands like `XREAD BLOCK` and `BLPOP` no longer hold up other commands on the shared connection. `NodeRedis`, `BunRedis` and `DenoRedis` layers accept `maxConnections`, `minConnections`, `idleTimeout` and `acquireTimeout` to size the pool.

  `Redis.make` now requires a `connect` option, and hand-written `Redis` services need a `reserve` member.

- [#10020](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10020) [`f09dc43`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/f09dc43db30175f6fafd43def2b96eff88e1a7fe) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow `FileSystem.makeTempDirectoryScoped` and `FileSystem.makeTempFileScoped` to finalize successfully when their temporary path has already been removed.
- Updated dependencies [[`ac3485a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ac3485a0ad2c5aa0c3d2f2c9f1b6739cb05b2a4e), [`3a4c691`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3a4c6919bd73b7564f8ef29dc5fe2e581289b52f), [`4f0600e`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f0600e90b61040c75b4c5c5be940db1079dd5f4),   - effect@4.0.7
  - @effect/platform-node-shared@4.0.7

# packages/platform/node-shared/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`66c796a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/66c796a089ff684613af6994d39fa64f4c11cedb) Thanks @effect-bot! - Bound Node stream and socket pulls to at most `chunkSize` chunks (default 4096), preventing synchronously refilling sources from draining indefinitely in one pull. Add a `chunkSize` option to Node socket constructors and a separate `readSize` option for Node stream `read(size)` calls; callers previously using `chunkSize` for byte read sizes should use `readSize` instead. Set `chunkSize: Infinity` to opt into unbounded batching.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`8f23668`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8f236683ad92bc4b4f58e16e91d40ce713c20578) Thanks @fubhy! - Replace `effect/Crypto` with family-specific `effect/crypto/*` services, opaque keys, `CryptoError`, a configurable `WebCrypto` reference and key schemas in `effect/Schema`. Encoding secret or private keys through byte/format schemas requires `{ allowEncode: true }`.

  ### Breaking changes

  - Use `SecureRandom` for randomness and UUIDs, and `Digest` with algorithm values such as `Digest.sha256` for hashing. Replace `effect/testing/TestCrypto` with `Layer.mock` for the required services.
  - Provide crypto explicitly; it is no longer included in the runtime `*Services` bundles. `NodeCrypto`, `BunCrypto`, `DenoCrypto` and `BrowserCrypto` offer individual family layers and a combined `layer`, supplying `SecureRandom` where needed. Core modules offer `layerWebCrypto`.
  - Provide `Digest.Digest` instead of `Crypto.Crypto` to `SqlMessageStorage` and `SingleRunner`, and instead of `EventLogEncryption` to `SqlEventLogServerEncrypted.makeStorage` and `layerStorage`. Replace `EventLogEncryption.sha256String` with `digest.digestHex(Digest.sha256, bytes)`.

  Existing workflow ID, activity idempotency key and event log table-name encodings are unchanged.

  Event log encryption, identity key derivation and session authentication now run on `Aead`, `Digest`, `Signature` and `SecureRandom`. `EventLogEncryption.make` and `EventLogEncryption.layer` take those services from context, so Node apps can use the `NodeCrypto` layers. `EventLogEncryption.layerSubtle` and the session auth functions use the Web Crypto layers on the `WebCrypto` reference instead of `globalThis.crypto`. Existing identities, derived signing keys and encrypted batches are unchanged.

  UUIDv7 and ULID generation is now monotonic below the maximum 48-bit timestamp, including within a millisecond and across clock rollback. Ordering is per `SecureRandom` instance; the unsafe helpers and model UUIDv7 defaults share per-realm ordering.

- [#10087](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10087) [`472c469`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/472c469ce125bc521f274dc4f5956cef5c26d430) Thanks @effect-bot! - Node.js 22.19.0 (required by `undici` 8) is now the minimum for `@effect/platform-node`, `@effect/platform-node-shared`, `@effect/opentelemetry` and `@effect/docgen`, up from 18.

  `NodeSocket` WebSocket clients now use Node's global `WebSocket` instead of `ws`, so client-only usage no longer bundles `ws`.

  **Breaking:** `ws` moved to a new `NodeWS` module.

  - `NodeSocket.NodeWS` and `BunSocket.NodeWS` are removed. Import `NodeWS` from `@effect/platform-node` or `@effect/platform-node-shared` (on Bun, import `ws` directly). Use `NodeWS.WebSocket` in place of `NodeSocket.NodeWS.default`.
  - `NodeSocket.layerWebSocketConstructorWS` is removed. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor`.

  **Behaviour changes** for clients that pass handshake headers, which previously used `ws`. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor` to keep the old behaviour:

  - Reads cannot be paused. Incoming frames are buffered without limit, and a configured `highWaterMark` fails the socket with a `SocketReadError`.
  - Connection errors are a generic `ErrorEvent`, so `SocketOpenError` causes lack the underlying error code.
  - The `Socket.WebSocket` service is a global `WebSocket`, so `ws` `instanceof` checks and `ws`-only methods such as `terminate()` and `ping()` no longer apply.
  - A custom `Host` header is ignored.
  - Outgoing messages are not compressed. Incoming `permessage-deflate` messages are still decompressed.
  - Connections use undici's global dispatcher instead of `http.globalAgent` / `https.globalAgent`.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`d2b6bc7`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/d2b6bc71ea63bc20f7efba052a95e71dc9853248) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Add experimental `Glob` path matching and a shared `FileSystem.glob` implementation for Node, Bun, and Deno.

  - Breaking: custom `FileSystem` implementations, including inputs to `FileSystem.make`, must provide `readDirectoryEntries`; mapping `readDirectory` names to `{ name, type: "Unknown" }` is supported. Wrappers must apply directory-listing changes to both methods.
  - `FileSystem.make` now derives `glob` instead of accepting it. Apply custom overrides afterward with `FileSystem.of({ ...fs, glob: customGlob })`.
  - Glob patterns use `/` separators and backslash escapes; Node extglobs no longer expand. Results use `/` separators and are relative to `root` unless the pattern is absolute. Deno wildcards now skip dotfiles unless explicitly named or `dot: true`; unreadable wildcard-reached directories are skipped, while read failures at the root or a leading literal directory propagate.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3d88452`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3d88452eb653a24fd8bd44894bedcdd8f3a09865) Thanks @fubhy! - Add `CommandLine` to `effect/process` for quoting and joining command lines, and support `Redacted` arguments and `env` values in `ChildProcess`.

  Migration:

  - `ChildProcess.make` templates keep quoted words together and throw a `CommandLineError` for shell syntax such as `|`, `$HOME` or `*`. Quote such characters or pass them with `${}`.
  - With the `shell` option, templates cannot contain `${}` values and extra arguments fail with `BadArgument`. Pass the whole script as the command, built with `CommandLine.join`.
  - `StandardCommand.args` and `env` values can be `Redacted` strings.
  - `ChildProcess.TemplateExpression` and `ChildProcess.TemplateExpressionItem` are removed. Use the `CommandLine` types of the same name.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`89e5443`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/89e5443280e15b3329f07cab3c03dc1f2ccc4ab0) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Add `Terminal.size`, a scoped queue that starts with the current terminal dimensions and receives each resize. Custom terminals passed to `Terminal.make` must now supply `size`.

### Patch Changes

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9795](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9795) [`958307b`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/958307b689b03cdb2ce2d27eb9041a485dd2fac0) Thanks @effect-bot! - Fail `ChildProcess` exit codes when a `stdin` or `additionalFds` input stream fails, instead of reporting a successful exit after truncated input.

- [#9823](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9823) [`5fbccf4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/5fbccf4a66afaa29f06331bbbbc9ca22a945b04a) Thanks @effect-bot! - Keep waiting for a detached `ChildProcess` process group that still exists but cannot be signalled, such as on macOS when only zombie members remain, instead of treating it as gone once the leader exits.

- [#9977](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9977) [`a3bd27a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/a3bd27ae09760755149875f64d0a3b17cfb854fb) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Make `NodeStream.toString` enforce `maxBytes` on the bytes read instead of the decoded text, which rejected non-UTF-8 input at the limit and accepted oversized UTF-16 input.

- [#9798](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9798) [`ec19ab3`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ec19ab308fd6dd278d2c20668102ab35fd38a5bb) Thanks @effect-bot! - Fix `NodeChildProcessSpawner` signalling a possibly reused process group after a non-detached child exits.

- [#10020](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10020) [`f09dc43`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/f09dc43db30175f6fafd43def2b96eff88e1a7fe) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow `FileSystem.makeTempDirectoryScoped` and `FileSystem.makeTempFileScoped` to finalize successfully when their temporary path has already been removed.

# packages/platform/node/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`8f23668`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8f236683ad92bc4b4f58e16e91d40ce713c20578) Thanks @fubhy! - Replace `effect/Crypto` with family-specific `effect/crypto/*` services, opaque keys, `CryptoError`, a configurable `WebCrypto` reference and key schemas in `effect/Schema`. Encoding secret or private keys through byte/format schemas requires `{ allowEncode: true }`.

  ### Breaking changes

  - Use `SecureRandom` for randomness and UUIDs, and `Digest` with algorithm values such as `Digest.sha256` for hashing. Replace `effect/testing/TestCrypto` with `Layer.mock` for the required services.
  - Provide crypto explicitly; it is no longer included in the runtime `*Services` bundles. `NodeCrypto`, `BunCrypto`, `DenoCrypto` and `BrowserCrypto` offer individual family layers and a combined `layer`, supplying `SecureRandom` where needed. Core modules offer `layerWebCrypto`.
  - Provide `Digest.Digest` instead of `Crypto.Crypto` to `SqlMessageStorage` and `SingleRunner`, and instead of `EventLogEncryption` to `SqlEventLogServerEncrypted.makeStorage` and `layerStorage`. Replace `EventLogEncryption.sha256String` with `digest.digestHex(Digest.sha256, bytes)`.

  Existing workflow ID, activity idempotency key and event log table-name encodings are unchanged.

  Event log encryption, identity key derivation and session authentication now run on `Aead`, `Digest`, `Signature` and `SecureRandom`. `EventLogEncryption.make` and `EventLogEncryption.layer` take those services from context, so Node apps can use the `NodeCrypto` layers. `EventLogEncryption.layerSubtle` and the session auth functions use the Web Crypto layers on the `WebCrypto` reference instead of `globalThis.crypto`. Existing identities, derived signing keys and encrypted batches are unchanged.

  UUIDv7 and ULID generation is now monotonic below the maximum 48-bit timestamp, including within a millisecond and across clock rollback. Ordering is per `SecureRandom` instance; the unsafe helpers and model UUIDv7 defaults share per-realm ordering.

- [#10087](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10087) [`472c469`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/472c469ce125bc521f274dc4f5956cef5c26d430) Thanks @effect-bot! - Node.js 22.19.0 (required by `undici` 8) is now the minimum for `@effect/platform-node`, `@effect/platform-node-shared`, `@effect/opentelemetry` and `@effect/docgen`, up from 18.

  `NodeSocket` WebSocket clients now use Node's global `WebSocket` instead of `ws`, so client-only usage no longer bundles `ws`.

  **Breaking:** `ws` moved to a new `NodeWS` module.

  - `NodeSocket.NodeWS` and `BunSocket.NodeWS` are removed. Import `NodeWS` from `@effect/platform-node` or `@effect/platform-node-shared` (on Bun, import `ws` directly). Use `NodeWS.WebSocket` in place of `NodeSocket.NodeWS.default`.
  - `NodeSocket.layerWebSocketConstructorWS` is removed. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor`.

  **Behaviour changes** for clients that pass handshake headers, which previously used `ws`. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor` to keep the old behaviour:

  - Reads cannot be paused. Incoming frames are buffered without limit, and a configured `highWaterMark` fails the socket with a `SocketReadError`.
  - Connection errors are a generic `ErrorEvent`, so `SocketOpenError` causes lack the underlying error code.
  - The `Socket.WebSocket` service is a global `WebSocket`, so `ws` `instanceof` checks and `ws`-only methods such as `terminate()` and `ping()` no longer apply.
  - A custom `Host` header is ignored.
  - Outgoing messages are not compressed. Incoming `permessage-deflate` messages are still decompressed.
  - Connections use undici's global dispatcher instead of `http.globalAgent` / `https.globalAgent`.

### Patch Changes

- [#10081](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10081) [`438ce72`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/438ce725e9be9b5b66b4b8d2b86c7b1b92fe15e6) Thanks @effect-bot! - `NodeHttpClient.layerNodeHttp` now encodes `FormData` request bodies with the global `Response`, so it no longer loads the `undici` package. Bundles that use the `node:http` client no longer include undici. File contents are still streamed as the upload needs them, and a file that cannot be read still fails the request with a `TransportError`. The Undici-backed client is unchanged.

- [#10071](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10071) [`19d36b8`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/19d36b8948fc1ae6c570382d47fb5e70bafba25f) Thanks @effect-bot! - Convert Node HTTP server request headers to `Headers` instead of casting them, so configured header redaction applies when request headers are logged or inspected.

- [#10065](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10065) [`8e668fe`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8e668fe1ceb53846df37797d3df692e02bc3f7c5) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading UTF-8 BOM in URL-encoded body field names.

- [#10067](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10067) [`ce9e999`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ce9e9990546e38a0fadbc64240c2eedbb1c353d6) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Preserve a leading `?` in URL-encoded form field names. `urlParamsBody` previously dropped it as a query-string prefix, so `?name=value` decoded to `name`.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9751](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9751) [`fb38ce6`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/fb38ce6b01af86a0fb9ac1ee23600005c021cbf5) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow buffered payloads and typed `101` response headers on `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade` endpoints.

  - `HttpApiSchema.Upgrade({ protocol, headers })` declares `101` headers, encoded like `WithHeaders` headers. Clients decode them and return `{ headers, socket }`, and OpenAPI lists them on the `101`.
  - Upgrade endpoints can declare buffered payloads. Streaming payloads are still rejected.
  - Handlers receive an `upgrade` function instead of `socket`: `upgrade()`, or `upgrade({ headers })` when headers are declared.
  - `426 Upgrade Required` is returned before the payload is decoded.
  - `HttpServerRequest` gains `upgradeWith({ headers })`, and `upgrade` is `upgradeWith({})`. Third-party `HttpServerRequest` implementations must add it. WebSocket upgrades fail when headers are given.
  - `NodeHttpServer` waits for the request body to end before writing the `101`. On Node 25 and older, which do not parse upgrade request bodies, it reads `Content-Length` bodies from the connection and answers chunked ones with `411 Length Required`.
  - `NodeMultipart` fails with a `MultipartError` when its source stream errors, instead of waiting for more input.

- [#10004](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10004) [`4ee3a67`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4ee3a6799f4188777dd7a7491763a88518278ae2) Thanks @athammer! - Fix `NodeHttpServer` hanging or reporting a defect when an HTTP/1 client disconnects before the response is written.

- [#9978](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9978) [`07ca583`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/07ca5835266720329795548d55958d9de4e11a88) Thanks @jpowersdev! - Fix `NodeMultipart.stream` hanging when the source closes early or is already destroyed, including any file part still being read, and remove its source listeners once parsing finishes.

- [#9828](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9828) [`45c467a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/45c467a106da66f785f2fd846aa3fad5c3dd2867) Thanks @tim-smart! - Add `Redis.reserve`, which leases a dedicated connection from a pool, so blocking commands like `XREAD BLOCK` and `BLPOP` no longer hold up other commands on the shared connection. `NodeRedis`, `BunRedis` and `DenoRedis` layers accept `maxConnections`, `minConnections`, `idleTimeout` and `acquireTimeout` to size the pool.

  `Redis.make` now requires a `connect` option, and hand-written `Redis` services need a `reserve` member.

# packages/sql/pg/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`4f16778`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/4f167781512ad6c1e5ac54bd4449237d12d0f96b) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add the unstable `Pool.Stats` and `Pool.stats` APIs for sampling how many pool items are idle or in use and how many borrowers are waiting. `PgPool.stats` and `PgClient.poolStats` expose the same snapshot for PostgreSQL sessions, so applications can export connection pool metrics without reaching into pool internals.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#10000](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10000) [`3f0343c`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3f0343cf5868a9781bc6c2229530da2f998f5a0e) Thanks @tim-smart! - Wait for an in-flight `UNLISTEN` when a pinned `PgConnection` is released, instead of retiring the connection as if it had leaked work.

- [#9756](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9756) [`cccf1ea`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/cccf1ea42c48c654b356841c7b3b09e57d2df9ef) Thanks @effect-bot! - Preserve the PostgreSQL error when the server reports a FATAL error, such as a transaction timeout, and then closes the connection, instead of failing with a generic connection-closed error.

- [#9996](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9996) [`7302cab`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/7302cabced4e7b855c3b3351970de7a7649b2df8) Thanks @effect-bot! - Fix a `PgConnection` stream started as its pin is released hanging instead of failing.

- [#9896](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9896) [`c1ba405`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/c1ba405edb75980aa44deb4194a078a2c6795aea) Thanks @wmaurer! - Fix closing a `listen` scope hanging when the connection has silently stopped responding. A session that does not confirm `UNLISTEN` within 5 seconds is now closed.

- [#9937](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9937) [`f722e4c`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/f722e4c4fb035ee49eb2b5c5ee85b81eb8fe1c25) Thanks @effect-bot! - Allow PostgreSQL statements with up to 65,535 bound parameters. `Parse` and `Bind` previously rejected more than 32,767 parameters with an `EncodeError`, although the protocol counts are unsigned 16-bit values.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9951](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9951) [`8450ad5`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/8450ad5be7d5c13489fdcf0170e9001c63a53f78) Thanks @tim-smart! - Run storage-backed transactions through `SqlClient.makeWithTransaction`.

  - `SqlResolver` keeps requests inside a transaction out of other batches, even without `SqlClient` in context.
  - `Reactivity` invalidations inside a transaction run after the outermost commit.
  - `afterCommit` callbacks run after the outermost commit.
  - Concurrent nested sibling transactions are serialized.
  - Transactions get a `sql.transaction` span.
  - Storage rejections during rollback or nested completion are defects, not `SqlError`s.
- Updated dependencies [[`ac3485a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ac3485a0ad2c5aa0c3d2f2c9f1b6739cb05b2a4e), # packages/sql/sqlite-react-native/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`be9895b`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/be9895bd7608f606dfdc4910ab722e2eb700ab19) Thanks @bezff! - Move `fromFileSystem` from `effect/sql/Migrator` to `effect/sql/MigratorFileSystem`, so React Native apps can import `effect/sql/Migrator` and `effect/sql` under Metro. The `effect/sql` barrel and `@effect/sql-sqlite-react-native/SqliteMigrator` no longer export `fromFileSystem`; the other SQL adapter migrators still re-export it.

# packages/tools/docgen/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#10087](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/10087) [`472c469`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/472c469ce125bc521f274dc4f5956cef5c26d430) Thanks @effect-bot! - Node.js 22.19.0 (required by `undici` 8) is now the minimum for `@effect/platform-node`, `@effect/platform-node-shared`, `@effect/opentelemetry` and `@effect/docgen`, up from 18.

  `NodeSocket` WebSocket clients now use Node's global `WebSocket` instead of `ws`, so client-only usage no longer bundles `ws`.

  **Breaking:** `ws` moved to a new `NodeWS` module.

  - `NodeSocket.NodeWS` and `BunSocket.NodeWS` are removed. Import `NodeWS` from `@effect/platform-node` or `@effect/platform-node-shared` (on Bun, import `ws` directly). Use `NodeWS.WebSocket` in place of `NodeSocket.NodeWS.default`.
  - `NodeSocket.layerWebSocketConstructorWS` is removed. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor`.

  **Behaviour changes** for clients that pass handshake headers, which previously used `ws`. Use `NodeWS.layerWebSocketConstructor` to keep the old behaviour:

  - Reads cannot be paused. Incoming frames are buffered without limit, and a configured `highWaterMark` fails the socket with a `SocketReadError`.
  - Connection errors are a generic `ErrorEvent`, so `SocketOpenError` causes lack the underlying error code.
  - The `Socket.WebSocket` service is a global `WebSocket`, so `ws` `instanceof` checks and `ws`-only methods such as `terminate()` and `ping()` no longer apply.
  - A custom `Host` header is ignored.
  - Outgoing messages are not compressed. Incoming `permessage-deflate` messages are still decompressed.
  - Connections use undici's global dispatcher instead of `http.globalAgent` / `https.globalAgent`.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`3d88452`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3d88452eb653a24fd8bd44894bedcdd8f3a09865) Thanks @fubhy! - Add `CommandLine` to `effect/process` for quoting and joining command lines, and support `Redacted` arguments and `env` values in `ChildProcess`.

  Migration:

  - `ChildProcess.make` templates keep quoted words together and throw a `CommandLineError` for shell syntax such as `|`, `$HOME` or `*`. Quote such characters or pass them with `${}`.
  - With the `shell` option, templates cannot contain `${}` values and extra arguments fail with `BadArgument`. Pass the whole script as the command, built with `CommandLine.join`.
  - `StandardCommand.args` and `env` values can be `Redacted` strings.
  - `ChildProcess.TemplateExpression` and `ChildProcess.TemplateExpressionItem` are removed. Use the `CommandLine` types of the same name.

# packages/tools/openapi-generator/CHANGELOG.md

## 4.1.0

### Minor Changes

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`611e3e3`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/611e3e3275eced2fd8091be107ae39ccddc99e8a) Thanks @ojhaujjwal! - Generated `httpclient` clients now keep the error and requirement types of the `HttpClient` passed to `make`, so errors and services added by client middleware appear in each operation's signature. The generated interface gains `E` and `R` type parameters that default to `HttpClientError` and `never`, so existing clients are unchanged.

- [#8829](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/8829) [`38f96d8`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/38f96d8154fa7deb5ce1be0cd9a63ad0e7ef39c6) Thanks @effect-bot! - Add an `includeSchemas` generation option that emits component schemas no operation references, and support the RFC 6902 `move` operation in OpenAPI patches.

## 4.0.7

### Patch Changes

- [#9836](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9836) [`baf5ca4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/baf5ca4bffffc2b1ca5cf6a78b21df6bc71726ae) Thanks @tim-smart! - Report defects raised by finalizers when Vitest cancels a test, for example on timeout. Previously only the timeout was shown and the finalizer defect was dropped.

- [#9832](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9832) [`678f153`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/678f153b1996ff07ff4af9b5bbcd42c9b9cdca6e) Thanks @tim-smart! - Add a `buildPerTest` option to `layer` and `it.layer` that builds the layer once per test, so selected services are recreated for each test while services from enclosing blocks stay shared.

- [#9930](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/pull/9930) [`3836ce4`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/3836ce44f3b712cc7fa8d01897be6803c11fef32) Thanks @Marve10s! - Fix unnamed `it.layer` blocks releasing their shared layer before later tests run when a test uses `retry` or `repeats`. Such blocks now keep their resources alive until the enclosing suite ends, so they may overlap with later blocks.
- Updated dependencies [[`ac3485a`](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/commit/ac3485a0ad2c5aa0c3d2f2c9f1b6739cb05b2a4e),
